    async def _check_raise_for_error(
        self, cell: NotebookNode, cell_index: int, exec_reply: dict[str, t.Any] | None
    ) -> None:
        if exec_reply is None:
            return None

        )

            self.on_cell_error, cell=cell, cell_index=cell_index, execute_reply=exec_reply
        )
